country_event = {
    id = 9901000
    title = "cve_opening_title"
    desc = "cve_opening_desc"
    picture = "cvi_florence"
    trigger = {
        tag = TIE
        ai = no
        has_country_flag = cvt_formed
        is_vassal = no
        NOT = {
            has_country_flag = cve_opening
        }
    }
    mean_time_to_happen = {
        days = 7
    }
    option = {
        name = "cve_opening_0"
        ai_chance = {
            factor = 100
            modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = {
                    AND = {
                        tag = TIE
                        ai = no
                        has_country_flag = cvt_formed
                        is_vassal = no
                        NOT = {
                            has_country_flag = cve_opening
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        random_owned = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    tag = TIE
                    ai = no
                    has_country_flag = cvt_formed
                    is_vassal = no
                    NOT = {
                        has_country_flag = cve_opening
                    }
                }
            }
            owner = {
                TIE = {
                    set_country_flag = cve_opening
                    plurality = 1
                    any_pop = {
                        militancy = -0.25
                    }
                    set_country_flag = cve_accommodation
                }
            }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = "cve_opening_1"
        ai_chance = {
            factor = 100
            modifier = {
                factor = 0
                NOT = {
                    AND = {
                        tag = TIE
                        ai = no
                        has_country_flag = cvt_formed
                        is_vassal = no
                        NOT = {
                            has_country_flag = cve_opening
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        random_owned = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    tag = TIE
                    ai = no
                    has_country_flag = cvt_formed
                    is_vassal = no

                        has_country_flag = cve_opening
